Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any heat module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 943. The projective resolution has length at most 473 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 943 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the heat structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 473. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
